Conclusion: AgenticECO enables attributable, low-disturbance automated ECOs for 3D-ICs with strong empirical validation and reproducible artifacts.

Abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [96] [MissClick: Exploiting Digit-Serialized Coordinates to Attack GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03740)
*Yu Ran,Wentao Zhao,Xin Zhang,Yi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Designs place-value-aware adversarial objectives (soft-coordinate displacement and place-weighted digit loss) for digit-token coordinate outputs, yielding large gains in untargeted and targeted hijacking of GUI grounding models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coordinate outputs are discrete digit tokens with place-value semantics; small token changes in high-place digits cause large numerical displacement. Prior attacks treated coordinates as text and ignored numeric/place-value structure and different untargeted vs targeted goals.

Method: Proposes MissClick, a white-box adversarial attack targeting GUI visual grounding models that output coordinates as digit-token sequences. Introduces two goal-specific optimization objectives: MissClick-U (maximizes soft-coordinate displacement for untargeted disruption) and MissClick-T (minimizes a place-weighted target-digit loss for targeted hijacking). Implements attacks against models on OS-Atlas and UGround across desktop, web, and mobile.

Result: MissClick-U substantially improves untargeted attack success (75.07% and 72.93%, +16.62 and +30.72 percentage points). MissClick-T substantially improves targeted success (44.86% and 62.67%, +31.73 and +47.06 pp) compared to prior attacks on OS-Atlas and UGround. Objective comparison shows soft-coordinate displacement best for untargeted and place-weighted digit loss best for targeted attacks.

Conclusion: Modeling numeric and place-value structure in coordinate-token outputs and using goal-specific objectives yields much more effective adversarial attacks; defenses should consider numeric parsing and tokenization behavior.

Abstract: Recent GUI visual grounding models generate screen coordinates as sequences of digit tokens that are parsed into numerical values and mapped to executable clicks. The security implications of this coordinate generation process have been largely overlooked. We observe that each coordinate digit is predicted as a categorical token, yet after parsing, changing a hundreds-place digit by one changes the corresponding numerical coordinate component by 100 units, which can induce a large displacement of the executed click. This observation motivates attack objectives that account for the numerical and place-value structure of coordinate outputs rather than treating them as ordinary text. Moreover, untargeted and targeted attacks impose different success conditions--displacing the click outside the correct region versus into an attacker-specified region--and therefore benefit from different objectives. We propose MissClick, a simple and effective white-box adversarial attack with two goal-specific objectives: MissClick-U maximizes soft-coordinate displacement for untargeted disruption, while MissClick-T minimizes a place-weighted target-digit loss for targeted hijacking. Compared with existing attacks against GUI grounding models on OS-Atlas and UGround across desktop, web, and mobile platforms, MissClick-U achieves untargeted success rates of 75.07\% and 72.93\% (+16.62 and +30.72 pp), and MissClick-T achieves targeted success rates of 44.86\% and 62.67\% (+31.73 and +47.06 pp). Attack objective comparison further shows that soft-coordinate displacement yields the highest untargeted attack success rate, whereas place-weighted target-digit optimization yields the highest targeted attack success rate, revealing distinct objective preferences for the two attack goals.

</details>


### [97] [Agents Catching Agents: Shortcut Cascades and Benchmark Gaming in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03744)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Agastya Munnangi,Aldo Marzullo,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Quang Bui,Mohammad Shahin,Armaan Grewal,Emmanuel Paul Kwesiga,Anqi Peter Li,Josephine Nanyonjo,Aaditya Panchal,Arshnoor Bhutani,Nikhil Jaiswal,Milit S. Patel,Maximin Lange,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多代理临床决策系统能被同伴一致的错误或虚假“预筛”信号所感染；只有独立、私下重新询问的裁判能较可靠地检测出被感染行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估多语言模型代理委员会在临床决策支持场景中是否会被基准奖励但临床上不相关的捷径、提示或社会线索所“游戏”，并比较不同监督策略的检测能力。

Method: 在六个公开数据集（文本：MedQA-USMLE、MedMCQA、MIMIC-CXR 报告；影像：NIH ChestX-ray14、MIMIC-CXR-JPG、CheXpert；表格：SUPPORT2）上构建七个不同队列的 Gemini 委员会。测量单一提示、同伴一致、虚假预筛标志、视觉显著性和多声部影响，评估三类监督代理（门、同系审判、私下复询裁判）对被感染答复的检测能力，并检查是否能从模型自述中回溯到被迁移的隐性评分标准。

Result: Committees of Gemini language-model agents largely resist isolated superficial cues (flip rate 5–16%), but exhibit strong social-contagion shortcuts: a holdout adopts a wrong answer when two peers assert it in 38% of cases, and similarly when given a false pre-screen flag. Oversight agents differ: a gate cannot distinguish adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a judge that sees only the transcript detects adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but fails in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout works across modalities (precision 77–88%, false-positive 13–21%). Increasing visual salience has no effect on contagion; adding a second peer voice increases contagion by ~50%. Hidden rubric gaming is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters explicitly name the rubric they shifted toward. Overall: social plausibility drives gaming and only an independent referee reliably detects it.

Conclusion: 社会可行性（同伴一致性）是使委员会被捷径化的主要因子；基于转录的审查在影像上失效，而门控无法区分真实共识与被感染，故应采用独立的再次询问机制并强化审计与对抗测试。

Abstract: Clinical decision support is moving toward committees of language-model agents deliberating on a shared workspace. We ask whether such committees can be gamed by shortcuts, cues a benchmark rewards but a clinician would ignore. Across seven cohorts on six public datasets spanning text (MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR reports), imaging (NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert) and tabular ICU records (SUPPORT2), Gemini committees resist these cues in isolation (flip 5-16%), yet a socially plausible shortcut spreads: when two peers assert the same wrong answer, the holdout under test adopts it in 38% of cases, as does a false "pre-screen" system flag, on both capability tiers. Of three oversight agents, a gate cannot separate adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a same-lineage judge reading only the transcript flags adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but collapses onto the gate in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout transfers to imaging (77-88% precision, 13-21% false-positive rate). Tripling a cue's visual salience does not move contagion, whereas a second peer voice raises it by half again. Gaming a hidden rubric is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters name the rubric they moved toward. What games a committee is social plausibility, and only a referee independent of self-report catches it. Code: https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

</details>


### [98] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用替换式推理干预（TRR）和HazMart数据集揭示了CoT监控中的忠实-安全权衡，定位了相应的内部表征并证明了表征引导能在不损失能力的前提下提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于CoT的监控依赖于模型输出真的来源于其内在推理轨迹（忠实性），但现实系统还需防止模型产生并执行不安全推理，作者旨在量化并研究二者间的权衡，提出更具因果性的测试与干预手段。

Method: 提出并使用了替换式的链式推理干预方法——Targeted Reasoning Replacement（TRR），构建人类编写的数据集HazMart用于自主AI店主场景评估；对比多个LRM的忠实性与安全性指标；进行机理学（mechanistic）分析以定位内部表征方向，并通过表征引导（representation steering）来放大安全方向。

Result: TRR能直接在推理链中插入不安全或不合逻辑的“想法”以测试忠实性。实验显示：DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B忠实性高（97.5%）但拒绝不安全推理能力低（12.3%）；QwQ-32B更安全（73.9%）但忠实性较低（74.7%）。机理分析发现代表两性状的内部方向呈反相关并在action-commit token处峰值；通过表征引导可以将安全方向放大，安全性提升约9个百分点且保持基础能力。

Conclusion: 存在一个对齐张力：模型需对链式推理输出保持充分的忠实性以便被监控，同时又要足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全或不合逻辑的推理。该张力在现有大推理模型中确实存在，但可通过表征级干预（representation steering）及替换式的干预测试得到部分缓解。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [99] [GDPevo: Evaluating Agent Self-Evolution on Real Business Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03764)
*Leijun Zhou,Zhihao Liu,Xiang Qu,Chenxu Liu,Yifei Liu,Yanke Yu,Jingzhe Xu,Xuejun Wu,Buyue Qian,Xi Chen,Yaowei Zheng,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GDPevo 是一个自动化生成、基于规则重组的企业工作流自演进基准，能更可靠地把测试收益归因于训练经验，实验证明自演进有效但现有代理仍远未达到上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演进评测基准覆盖领域有限、训练/测试设计不能保证归因性且易受数据污染，亟需一个可扩展、可自动生成且与企业 GDP 相关的基准来真实评估长期学习与知识重用的能力。

Method: 构建自动化数据管道，采用“规则混合”（rule hybridization）：把每个企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，在训练任务中分配规则子集，并在持出测试任务中重组合这些规则；提供跨 CRM、ERP、财务、医疗、法律与数据相关工作流的任务集合；评估四种代理（由 harness 与 model 组成）在四类监督方式下的自演进效果。

Result: V1 包含 12 组共 120 个任务（每组 5 个训练+5 个持出测试）；管道能在两天内扩展至 V2 的 24 组 240 个任务。实验显示自演进能使持出任务准确率最多提升 16.44 个百分点，但最佳演化后代理仍显著低于 91.6% 的告知型上界。基准、管道与完整评测结果已公开。

Conclusion: 作者提出了面向企业 GDP 工作流的自演进评测基准 GDPevo，通过规则混合将业务规则原子化后在训练/测试间重组，从而使测试收益可归因于训练经验，实验表明自演进能显著提高准确率但仍远低于告知型上界。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution updates an agent's persistent state from prior experience and reuses it to solve related tasks more effectively. Evaluating self-evolution is difficult: existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of economically valuable task domains, do not always design training and test tasks such that test-time gains can be attributed to training experience, and remain vulnerable to data contamination. We present GDPevo, an evolution-native benchmark grounded in GDP-related enterprise workflows, together with the fully automated data pipeline that generates it. Its core mechanism, rule hybridization, decomposes each enterprise workflow into atomic business rules, distributes subsets of these rules across training tasks, and recombines them in held-out test tasks so that test-time gains are attributable. GDPevo spans CRM, ERP, finance, healthcare, legal, and data-centric workflows. Its V1 release contains 120 tasks in 12 groups, with five training and five held-out test tasks per group. Full automation enables the pipeline to expand the suite to 240 tasks in 24 groups (V2) within two days, providing a practical response to contamination. Using GDPevo, we evaluate four agents, each comprising a harness and a model, under four supervision types. Self-evolution consistently improves held-out accuracy by up to 16.44 percentage points. But the best evolved agents remain far below the fully informed oracle ceiling of 91.6%, indicating that the self-evolution ability of current agents remains far from fully realized. We publicly release the pipeline, benchmark, and full evaluation results at https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo.

</details>


### [100] [Computing Actual Causes for Neural Network Predictions under Structured Causal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03772)
*Jannick Strobel,Muqsit Azeem,Stefan Leue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将解释问题形式化为HP实际因果并在布尔SCM上求解，提出基于约束传播与分支定界的带保证算法，显著提升可扩展性并减少因为忽视依赖产生的虚假原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing explanation methods treat input features as independent, producing misleading explanations when inputs have structured dependencies. The paper formalizes explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes and models input dependencies with Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs).

Method: 用布尔结构因果模型表征输入依赖，按Halpern-Pearl定义编码实际因果条件；采用界限传播（bound propagation）进行剪枝，结合分支定界搜索（branch-and-bound）枚举候选（cause, contingency）对，提供完备性和极小性证明；在合成与案例研究中与暴力、ILP和启发式基线比较性能。

Result: A practical algorithm combining bound propagation and branch-and-bound computes all minimal HP causes with formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Empirically it outperforms brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, scales to SCMs with up to 28 nodes and search spaces up to 2.3e13 candidate pairs within 180s, and shows that 14.9% of reported causes are spurious if dependencies are ignored.

Conclusion: 在布尔SCM框架下，准确建模输入依赖并用可证明的算法枚举最小实际因果，能得到更可靠、更精简的解释；方法受限于SCM准确性与布尔变量假设，未来工作可扩展到连续/概率化模型。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural networks is a central challenge in trustworthy AI. Existing explanation methods, such as those based on feature attribution or minimal sufficient sets, typically treat input features as independent, which can yield misleading explanations when inputs exhibit structured dependencies. We address this by formalizing explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes, modeling input dependencies using Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We compute HP causes by applying bound propagation and branch-and-bound techniques, while providing formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Our experiments show that we substantially outperform brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, and outperform heuristic search as graph size grows, computing all minimal actual causes on instances with search spaces of up to $2.3\times10^{13}$ candidate (cause, contingency) pairs, on SCMs with up to 28 nodes, within a 180s per-instance budget. In a case study, we further show that ignoring input dependencies inflates the number of reported causes, 14.9% of which are spurious under our SCM.

</details>


### [101] [KnowHal: A Knowledge-Driven Benchmark for Comprehensive Multimodal Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03782)
*Ruihan Li,Jiyang Tan,Kailin Jiang,Huining Li,Hengyang Lu,Yu Huang,Qian Li,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowHal通过将知识幻觉纳入并与实体/属性/关系幻觉并列，以成对正负问题设计和半自动构建流程，提供了更全面的多模态幻觉评估，实验证明知识相关幻觉与对错误前提的脆弱性是当前MLLM的主要短板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）存在的幻觉问题，尤其是知识相关幻觉，在现有基准中未与实体、属性、关系幻觉统一评估，缺乏跨维度的一致性评价框架。

Method: 半自动构建：用LLM生成候选正负问题—基于共享图像与实体设计配对问题；用CLIP等视觉语言检索/过滤不匹配样本；人工进行最终筛查与标注。对比评估14个MLLM，按四个维度分析错误模式与鲁棒性。

Result: 提出KnowHal基准：覆盖实体、属性、关系、知识四个幻觉维度；采用共享图像与实体的正负问题对设计；数据集包含1800个样本、10个领域、50类；通过LLM辅助、CLIP筛选与人工验证的半自动流水线构建；在14个代表性MLLM上评估，发现知识维度最具挑战性、负样本对模型鲁棒性影响显著。

Conclusion: KnowHal填补了多模态幻觉评估中知识维度和统一评估框架的空白，为衡量模型在不同幻觉类型及对假前提出错的鲁棒性提供了标准化工具，提示未来需在知识对齐与假前提识别上加强模型能力。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a critical challenge for developing trustworthy Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing benchmarks mainly focus on entity, attribute, and relation hallucinations, knowledge-related failures are often investigated separately, lacking a unified evaluation framework across different hallucination dimensions. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{KnowHal}, a benchmark that explicitly incorporates knowledge hallucination into multimodal hallucination evaluation spanning four dimensions: entity, attribute, relation, and knowledge. KnowHal constructs paired positive and negative questions over shared images and entities, enabling controlled comparisons among perceptual errors, knowledge-related errors, and false-premise acceptance. The benchmark contains 1,800 samples across 10 domains and 50 categories, constructed through a semi-automated pipeline combining LLM assistance, CLIP-based filtering, and human verification. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs on KnowHal and conduct extensive analyses. Results show that the knowledge dimension consistently presents the greatest challenge for nearly all evaluated models, while most models exhibit substantial performance degradation on negative questions, revealing limited robustness to false premises. By unifying four hallucination dimensions with paired question design, KnowHal addresses an important gap in existing evaluation frameworks and enables a more comprehensive assessment of hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [102] [Does Forgetting Transfer Across Modalities? A Real-World Benchmark for Cross-Modal Knowledge Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03791)
*Chunlin Liu,Junnian Chen,Haitong Jiang,Jianyu Zhao,Yingsen Pang,Jingchen Li,Jiabiao He,Youming Lu,Jinhe Bi,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者构建UNLINK-VL基准以研究视觉-语言模型的跨模态知识去学习。实验显示文本去学习难以迁移到视觉/跨模态，而多模态去学习在文本上有效，表明必须进行跨模态去学习与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要在单一模态内研究知识去学习，而视觉-语言模型在预训练中可能记忆敏感或受版权/有害信息，需要研究跨模态知识去学习及其评估，以避免仅靠单模态评估导致效果高估。

Method: 提出UNLINK-VL基准：在post-hoc unlearning设定下（原始忘记/保留语料不可用），以可视辨识的真实实体为忘记目标，关联对应图像及来自Wikidata的一跳和多跳事实，构建四个子集评估直接遗忘、关系传播遗忘、相关非目标知识保留及对语义等价查询的鲁棒性；在文本仅与多模态两种去学习设置下训练模型，并在文本、视觉与跨模态场景下评估遗忘效果与保留效用。

Result: 大量实验显示明显的跨模态不对称性：多模态去学习在文本评估下仍然有效，而仅文本去学习很难迁移到视觉与跨模态场景；评估方法在大多数情况下能保持模型的一般能力。结论是仅依赖单模态（尤其文本）评估会高估去学习效果，强调需要跨模态的去学习与评估方法。

Conclusion: 应在实际VLM安全部署中采用跨模态去学习策略与跨模态评估；未来工作应开发能在各模态间有效传播忘却的去学习算法、考虑知识图谱传播效应并保证语义等价查询下的鲁棒性，同时维持模型下游能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), like Large Language Models (LLMs), may memorize sensitive, copyrighted, or harmful knowledge from their pretraining corpora. Removing such knowledge is essential for building trustworthy AI systems. However, existing studies primarily focus on forgetting within individual modalities. Although recent work has begun to explore cross-modal consistency in unlearning, the cross-modal transfer of real-world knowledge unlearning remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce UNLINK-VL, a real-world benchmark for cross-modal knowledge unlearning in VLMs. Under a post-hoc unlearning setting in which the original forget and retain corpora are unavailable, UNLINK-VL selects visually identifiable real-world entities as unlearning targets and associates them with corresponding images and one-hop and multi-hop facts derived from Wikidata. The benchmark comprises four complementary subsets that evaluate direct forgetting of target knowledge, the propagation of forgetting through relational knowledge, the preservation of related non-target knowledge, and robustness to semantically equivalent queries. We train models under text-only and multimodal unlearning settings and evaluate forgetting effectiveness and retained utility across textual, visual, and cross-modal scenarios. Extensive experiments reveal a pronounced asymmetry in cross-modal transfer: multimodal unlearning remains effective under textual evaluation, whereas text-only unlearning transfers poorly to visual and cross-modal scenarios. Meanwhile, the evaluated methods largely preserve the models' general capabilities. These findings demonstrate that relying solely on intra-modal evaluation, particularly text-only evaluation, may substantially overestimate the effectiveness of knowledge unlearning in VLMs, underscoring the need for cross-modal unlearning and evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把显式文本推理移到连续潜态以降低推理成本，同时通过独立解码器按需恢复可审计理由，兼顾效率与可检查性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式生成推理理由（rationales）可改进安全判断且易审计，但代价高昂；所以希望将推理迁移到连续潜态以降低生成成本，同时保留可审计性以满足部署与合规需求。

Method: 使用分阶段课程训练将任务对齐的文本理由压缩到紧凑连续向量；在推理时直接从潜态预测安全判决；另设独立的辅助解码器用于按需生成可审计的短文本工件，不进入标准推理路径。

Result: LatentGuard 提出用连续潜在表征进行守卫模型推理，并通过分阶段课程学习将文本推理压缩为紧凑潜态，同时保留可审计性（隔离的辅助解码器按需生成稽核工件）。实验证明在 8B 模型上在 F1 与推理成本上均有改善。

Conclusion: LatentGuard 在保持审计能力的前提下，显著降低了关键路径上的生成成本并略微提升了加权 F1，展示了将潜在推理用于安全守卫的可行性与部署潜力。

Abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [104] [Oilbird: Training-Free Speculative Decoding with Keys the Verifier Already Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03839)
*Tao Jin,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Zhenzhu Yan,Teeradaj Racharak,Naoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Exact suffix matching misses reachable correct drafts due to addressing limits. Re-keying the draft pool by verifier hidden states (semantic drafts) and merging them with lexical drafters substantially increases accepted generated length and end-to-end decoding speed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Address failures of training-free speculative decoding that drafts continuations by exact suffix matches from a pool of past contexts—fails notably on tool-calling traffic where almost-entire requests repeat except for a few minted values, and where a single rejected token can discard the correct continuation. The goal is to fix misses caused by addressing limitations rather than pool coverage.

Method: Empirical diagnosis position-by-position across ten benchmarks to show addressing failures; construct a semantic draft source by storing entries keyed by verifier hidden states at each committed token; design a merge mechanism so semantic drafts can be used inside a lexical drafter’s tree; evaluate with three published drafters on matched pools and budgets, measure accepted length and end-to-end decoding speed (API-Bank) against strong baselines.

Result: A second, semantic draft source that re-keys the same pool by the verifier’s hidden state at each committed token and a merge allowing it to integrate into an existing lexical drafter’s tree. Across three published drafters at matched pool and budget, this raises accepted length by 24–29%. The proposed system (Oilbird) achieves 4.4× autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank versus 3.9× for the best training-free baseline and 2.0× for EAGLE-3.

Conclusion: The main failure mode is addressing, not coverage. Semantic re-keying of a draft pool by verifier hidden states recovers many previously unreachable correct drafts and yields substantial efficiency gains in speculative decoding without training.

Abstract: Training-free speculative decoding drafts by matching an exact suffix of the context against a pool of earlier context. That lookup misses correct drafts already in the pool, most visibly on tool-calling traffic, where a request repeats almost everything but the few values minted for it, and where one rejected token discards the correct continuation behind it. We diagnose the failure position by position across ten benchmarks and find it to be a problem of addressing rather than of coverage: on our densest tool-calling benchmark, about half of what the strongest exact-match drafter misses is present in the pool yet unreachable by exact matching. We therefore propose a second, semantic draft source: the same pool, re-keyed by the hidden state the verifier has already computed at each committed token, together with a merge that lets it ride inside an existing lexical drafter's tree. In three published drafters, at matched pool and budget, it lifts accepted length by 24-29%. Oilbird reaches 4.4x autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank, against 3.9x for the strongest training-free baseline in our harness and 2.0x for EAGLE-3.

</details>


### [105] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAFIA提出了在大规模记忆库与审计机制下仍可奏效的查询仅记忆投毒框架，核心在于检索竞争性的投放策略与语义上被掩盖的事实型有效负载，能在实验中同时做到高成功率与躲避审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于记忆的LLM代理被用于长程推理与决策，持久化记忆成为敌对注入的攻击面。现有攻击在面对大规模无害记忆干扰和主动语义审计时效果下降，需设计新的查询仅攻击以评估并揭示这些系统的安全风险。

Method: 高层：查询仅场景下，先通过记忆探针定位检索竞争力高的位置（placement），并在预算与时间上进行分配与调度；再在注入的记录中插入“紧凑的事实性伪装”（factual cloaks），以在语义上保持与目标近似同时规避审计判定，从而在检索和审计两端取得成功。

Result: 总体上，本文提出的MAFIA在一个更现实的威胁模型下对记忆投毒攻击进行了扩展：针对大规模良性记忆池与主动语义审计场景，作者通过“探 probing + 事实注入”实现查询仅攻击，并报告了高成功率与低审计检测率。实验结果（最高90.7%成功率、审计检测从83.3%降至≤7.4%）显示出显著影响力。

Conclusion: 论文选题切中痛点，方法设计合理且实验数据有说服力，但仍需补充更多现实性细节（检索/审计实现、基线对比、白盒/黑盒假设）、细化消融实验，以及讨论防御与伦理发布策略，方可提高可信度与可复现性。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [106] [ADMITBench: A Safety-Governed Reference Framework for Evaluating the Admissibility of Industrial LLM Advisories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03866)
*Yash Misra,Javal Vyas,Siddharth Gutta,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADMITBench通过版本化的评估契约和工厂配置文件，在动作级别对工业LLM建议进行可审计的、显式不折衷的合规性与后果检查；0.1.0为研究用参考实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏面向动作层面的、可版本化与可审计的工业LLM建议评估框架；需要一种将证据、权限与工厂后果明确编码并自动化验证的机制，以降低模型导出的操作性风险并支持研发与合规审查。

Method: 制定并实现一个版本化评估契约，包含三类显式检查：证据支持检查、权限/程序允许性检查，以及基于工厂配置文件的后果可接受性检查。采用‘安全治理’一词指明合格性由非折衷（non-compensatory）的规则集合判定；提供公开的0.1.0参考实现用于技术/研究评估。

Result: ADMITBench实现了一个版本化的、“安全治理”的评估契约，用于按“拟采取动作”粒度验证工业LLM建议：检查建议是否有证据支持、是否根据声明的权限与程序被允许、以及是否在所选评估配置文件中编码的工厂特定后果检查下可接受。0.1.0为面向技术与研究评估的公开参考实现，并非用于现场执行或安全认证。

Conclusion: ADMITBench可为工业场景中的LLM建议提供一致、可审计的技术评估路径，有助于发现模型在证据支持、权限合规及工厂后果方面的缺陷，但并不等同于安全认证——其有效性依赖于工厂配置文件的完整性、检查规则的覆盖度与实际试验验证。

Abstract: This white paper presents ADMITBench, a reference framework for evaluating industrial LLM advisories at the level of the proposed action. The framework implements a versioned, safety-governed evaluation contract that checks whether a recommendation is supported by the available evidence, permitted under the stated authority and procedure, and acceptable under the plant-specific consequence checks encoded in the selected evaluation profile. In this report, \emph{safety-governed} means that eligibility is determined through explicit, non-compensatory checks derived from a versioned plant profile; it does not mean that the evaluator, model, or plant has been safety-certified. Release 0.1.0 is a public reference implementation for technical and research evaluation, not an authorisation for physical execution.

</details>


### [107] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ContinualSkillBench评测在上下文持续技能学习，发现顺序训练有益但收效不稳；上下文学习与显式技能维护总体相当，显式技能在特定场景有优势；弱模型更易产生碎片化技能，说明难以实现稳健的技能巩固与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架通过外部技能库扩展大语言模型以应对复杂任务，但尚不清楚这些系统在持续运行中能否有效演化技能，以及演化出的技能是否能提升解决任务的能力。因此需要一个动态评估平台来检验“在上下文内的持续技能学习（in‑context continual skill learning）”的有效性与可迁移性。

Method: 构建ContinualSkillBench：五个领域×各100个按难度排序的互联子任务，模拟持续学习场景；对比不同大语言模型与两种技能使用策略（仅在上下文中适配 vs 显式维护技能库）；测量序列执行带来的增益、技能可复用性、模型能力与技能碎片化程度。

Result: 提出ContinualSkillBench：一个包含5个代表性领域、每个领域100个按难度递增并具有跨任务可复用机会的连贯子任务的动态评估框架。实验结果表明：顺序执行通常能提升性能，但提升大小在模型与领域间差异显著；平均来看，在上下文学习与显式技能维护表现相近，说明改进主要来自对先前上下文与反馈的适配，而非完全依赖可复用技能抽象；显式技能在需要可复用程序或精确输出的任务上有选择性优势；能力较弱的模型更倾向于积累大量、碎片化的任务特定技能。总体结论是当前的在上下文技能演化机制能支持持续适配，但难以将经验稳定整合为健壮且可迁移的技能。

Conclusion: 当前基于上下文的技能演化能带来短期适配收益，但缺乏一致的技能巩固与可迁移性。未来需要机制来促进技能抽象、合并与压缩，避免模型依赖零散的任务特定片段。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [108] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用对比线性探针在残差流中寻找短期-长期方向，随后通过对比激活添加 steering 在多个任务上双向改变模型的时间偏好并改善中等程度的规划表现，表明模型的跨期偏好可被度量与可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并控制大型语言模型在时间维度上的偏好和决策倾向：这既能改进给出有关延迟成本与收益建议的系统的可靠性，也对评估与缓解模型在长时规划与安全性方面的潜在风险具有重大意义。

Method: 1) 使用教师强制（teacher-forced）的时间选择答案构建对比学习数据；2) 在模型残差流上训练对比线性探针以得到‘短期 vs 长期’方向；3) 在验证集中用对比激活添加（contrastive activation-addition）进行 steer，评估持出二元时间选择任务；4) 进行OOD 货币跨期选择实验（变化奖励大小与延迟）并测量无差异阈值变化；5) 在 TravelPlanner 能力基准上测试中等强度时间引导下的规划性能变化。

Result: 在 Qwen3-32B 的残差流中能找到线性“时间视野”方向，利用对比线性探针训练和激活添加（activation-addition）引导可实现双向、显著的时间偏好改变，并在货币延期选择与行程规划能力上表现出迁移与能力提升。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：Qwen3-32B 中存在可线性解码的时间视野表征，这些表征可被用于激活级别的干预，从而可测量并操控模型的跨期偏好与部分与时间相关的能力，为与延迟成本/收益相关的建议系统与长时规划安全性提供了可操作的工具与研究方向。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [109] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文分析了自适应Transformer跟踪器中的拓扑脆弱性，指出门控决策在离散跳层边界处存在Lipschitz奇异性，导致网络对微小输入扰动的拓扑路径选择极不稳定，并提出了对抗路径反演(API)攻击框架，通过不可觉察的扰动精确操控门控决策，从而强制模型走上改变的计算路径，破坏模型表示能力。实验表明API在隐蔽性、攻击效果与速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV等资源受限平台需要在精度与效率之间权衡，自适应Transformer跟踪器通过动态路由实现按需计算，但这种输入依赖的门控机制可能带来拓扑不稳定性与安全性隐患，本文旨在探究并证明这种弱点并构建有效攻击。

Method: 本文形式化刻画了门控决策的Lipschitz奇异性，定义了基于路径空间的攻击目标，设计了Adversarial Path-Inversion (API)框架以生成最小可觉察扰动精确翻转门控决策并强制改变推理拓扑；在逻辑上结合路径不一致度量和梯度优化来求解扰动，同时保证扰动隐蔽性和计算效率。

Result: 在多款现有自适应跟踪器上，API展示了更低的扰动幅度、更高的攻击成功率和更快的攻击推理速度，验证了路径空间攻击的致命性与实用性，提出对动态跟踪网络安全性的新警示。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了自适应跟踪器的一个新的攻击面——基于拓扑路径选择的Lipschitz奇异性，并提出了API攻击框架，实验证明其能有效且隐蔽地操控门控模块，破坏跟踪性能，提示未来需要在架构设计中考虑此类拓扑脆弱性以提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [110] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张用社会学理论把“多元对齐”从输出多样化升级为对社会化协调机制的工程化设计，提出角色化与结构化协调的具体系统要素，并呼吁实证实现与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在多样社会情境中的部署，对齐不应只优化单一价值集合；系统需识别、表征并响应多种合法视角。现有方法往往忽视价值如何在社会中被组织、争论和协调，因而需要社会理论提供概念与设计资源。

Method: 理论-设计映射：基于社会学理论将“视角”建模为角色（roles）、互动过程与领域化分布，并据此提出技术设计要素——基于角色的表示、结构化协调机制、情境敏感评估，以及对具代理性的系统要求（角色激活、审议痕迹、聚合规则、反馈回路）。同时构建一个面向多视角参与的设计空间并指明实证评估方向。

Result: 提出将多元对齐重新定位为“有社会根基的协调”问题，给出具体设计启示（角色化表示、结构化视角协调、情境化评估），并为具代理性的系统提出需对中间决策机制进行对齐的要求；最终输出为一个可导航的设计空间及若干未来研究方向以落实与实证这些方法。

Conclusion: 把多元对齐视为社会化的协调工程，而非单纯的输出多样化；为AI设计者提供了以角色、互动与场域分布为核心的概念工具与可操作化设计要素，并指出需要通过实地原型与评估来检验这些思想。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [111] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Implemented Álvarez & Ruggieri (2025) causal perception framework with algorithms for interventions and counterfactuals, distance-based disagreement measures, and an empirical case study showing metric/threshold sensitivity and model-dependent fairness outcomes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Different agents hold competing SCMs yielding different (interventional/counterfactual) distributions; need a concrete implementation to study how these differences affect reasoning, decisions and perceived fairness.

Method: Operationalize structural vs parametrical causal perception; compute interventional and counterfactual distributions with designed algorithms; define/choose distance measures to quantify disagreement; apply pipeline to German Credit dataset in multi-expert decision experiments and analyze accuracy and fairness effects.

Result: First implementation of the causal perception framework; operationalized structural and parametric disagreements; designed algorithms for interventional and counterfactual distributions; proposed distance measures to quantify disagreement; experiments on German Credit showing perceptual verdict sensitive to distance metric/threshold; causal perception impacts fairness and decisions; bias depends on agent's SCM.

Conclusion: Causal perception can be operationalized; choice of distance metric and threshold critically affects verdicts; competing SCMs lead to different fairness assessments and decisions, so fairness is model-dependent and multiple worldviews should be considered.

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [112] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transformers may implement Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing (SIDPP): they build and apply transformations whose parameters are generated from the input, explaining prompt sensitivity and suggesting links to cortex processing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Propose a new inference-time interpretation of Transformers: they construct prompt-dependent parameterized transformations during inference (SIDPP) rather than just reproducing statistical patterns.

Method: Conceptual interpretation mapping Transformer components to groups of small networks producing parameterized linear transformations; highlights output-weight interconnections that let some outputs determine other networks' weights; argues via qualitative analysis about scaling with prompt length and implications.

Result: A conceptual account where token vectors are concepts and parameterized transformations (static or dynamic) are constructed via output-weight interconnections; dynamic processing contribution grows with prompt length (strong prompt sensitivity); possible neural plausibility and implications for interpretability and model design.

Conclusion: SIDPP offers a framework for understanding dynamic, prompt-dependent computation in Transformers and conjectures a neurally realizable analogue in human cortex; empirical validation and mechanistic detail are needed.

Abstract: This paper offers a new interpretation of the Transformer during inference. Against the "stochastic parrot" view that large language models merely reproduce statistical regularities learned in training, we argue that Transformers construct and apply prompt-dependent transformations whose parameters are generated during inference. We call this form of computation SIDPP: Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing. The Transformer is interpreted as a system that transforms concepts by means of concepts. Token vectors are the concepts to be transformed; parameterized transformations defined by matrices and vectors are the transforming concepts. These may be static, when fixed through training, or dynamic, when generated from the input sequence. Mechanically, they correspond to groups of simple neural networks. The Transformer's architectural novelty lies in output-weight interconnections, through which the outputs of some networks determine the weights of others, alongside ordinary output-input interconnections. By means of these interconnections, the system constructs transformations from the prompt and uses them to modify token representations. The contribution of dynamic processing grows with prompt length and may equal or exceed that of static processing, a phenomenon we call strong prompt sensitivity. This account bears on interpretability, predictability, control, and the design of smaller, more sustainable systems. Finally, since the human neural system possesses the mechanisms required to implement SIDPP, we argue that a form of SIDPP may, in principle, be neurally realized in the cerebral cortex. We therefore conjecture that human language processing may itself be a form of SIDPP produced by a functional architecture relevantly similar to that of the Transformer.

</details>


### [113] [TACT: Taxonomy-Aligned Post-Training for Pedagogically Adaptive English Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03952)
*Dongjie Yang,Siyan Lin,Leixian Shen,Rui Sheng,Huamin Qu,Zixin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TACT = taxonomies + annotated corpus + post-training (SFT + policy optimization) -> more pedagogically adaptive ESL tutor with empirical improvements and released resources; promising but requires further validation and scaling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM tutors generate fluent responses but lack principled, pedagogically-adaptive action selection; existing human-tutoring insights are fragmented and not well integrated into LLM training/evaluation.

Method: Develop two taxonomies (13-strategy Tutor-Strategy and Student-Move taxonomy), annotate 260 authentic teacher-student dialogs into TACTCorpus (32,379 annotations + augmented data), then post-train Qwen3.5-4B via supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization to produce TACTutor optimized for scaffolding quality.

Result: On a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark (TACTBench, 78 contexts) TACTutor improves 20.30% over backbone and beats proprietary baselines under same protocol, keeps backbone performance on external educational benchmarks, and obtained highest mean rating in a blinded 50-learner study. Data, benchmark, and model weights are released.

Conclusion: TACT provides a human-grounded, taxonomy-aligned framework that meaningfully improves pedagogical adaptivity of LLM-based ESL tutors and supplies data/benchmarks/models to the community.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to provide conversational practice for English-as-a-second-language (ESL) learners. Effective ESL tutoring, however, requires more than fluent response generation: a tutor must select an appropriate pedagogical action based on learner behavior and dialogue context. Human-tutoring research offers principles for adaptive support, but they are often task-specific and remain insufficiently integrated into LLM-based ESL tutor training and evaluation. We present TACT (Taxonomy-Aligned Conversational Tutor), a human-grounded framework for post-training and evaluating pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors. Drawing on established literature, we develop two complementary taxonomies: the Tutor-Strategy Taxonomy with 13 tutor response strategies and the Student-Move Taxonomy characterizing learner behavior by move type and status. Using these taxonomies, we construct TACTCorpus, which enriches 260 authentic teacher-student conversations with 32,379 annotations and quality-controlled augmented training data. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through supervised fine-tuning followed by taxonomy-aligned Group Relative Policy Optimization, producing TACTutor and optimizing it for scaffolding quality rather than reference imitation alone. On TACTBench, a strategy-balanced diagnostic benchmark comprising 78 authentic tutoring contexts, TACTutor improves over its backbone by 20.30% and outperforms all evaluated proprietary baselines under the same protocol, while maintaining backbone performance on established external educational benchmarks; in a blinded study with 50 learners, it also receives the highest overall mean rating among the evaluated tutors. We release the data, benchmark, and model weights, providing an open foundation for developing pedagogically adaptive ESL tutors.

</details>


### [114] [A game theory for foundation models shows new paths to rational cooperation through similarity inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03958)
*Alexander Meulemans,Maciej Wołczyk,Marissa A. Weis,Rajai Nasser,Roberta Rocca,Seijin Kobayashi,Guillaume Lajoie,Angelika Steger,Blake Richards,Marcus Hutter,James Manyika,Rif A. Saurous,João Sacramento,Blaise Agüera y Arcas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当智能体在规划中把自己纳入模型并对自身决策保有不确定性时，它们会把自身选择视为对他人选择的证据，从而在社会困境中更容易实现稳定合作；由此构建了“嵌入贝叶斯主体”与“嵌入均衡”的理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于基础模型的自主智能体日益融入社会与经济系统，理解它们集体行为的基本原理对于安全与合作至关重要。经典博弈论因假定“解耦主体性”而难以描述现代AI主体在决策时同时预测自身未来动作的情形，需要新的理论框架。

Method: 在样式化社会困境中对基于基础模型的规划智能体进行实验并观察行为模式；提出并数学化“嵌入贝叶斯主体”的描述，形式化相似性推断，将该机制定义为“嵌入均衡”。

Result: 在样式化的社会困境中，使用最优规划的基础模型智能体一致收敛到稳定合作，违背了经典博弈论关于相互背弃的预测。提出“嵌入贝叶斯主体”理论模型，展示主体通过推断他人是否行为相似，将自身的推理视为证据，从而导致合作。引入“嵌入均衡”作为替代纳什均衡的新解概念，为现代AI主体的社会行为建立基础性博弈论框架。

Conclusion: 通过从解耦主体性转向嵌入主体性，并形式化相似性推理机制，可解释并预测现代基础模型智能体在社会困境中的合作倾向。嵌入均衡为分析此类系统提供了更适切的解概念，具有重要的安全与政策含义。

Abstract: As autonomous agents powered by foundation models are increasingly integrated into social and economic systems, understanding the principles governing their collective behavior is essential for ensuring safety and cooperation. Classical game theory, the dominant framework for modeling rational interaction, is built upon the assumption of `decoupled agency,' where agents treat their own decision-making as independent of the environment and other actors. Modern AI agents, however, jointly predict their own future actions alongside external observations. Here, we report a striking finding: when interacting in stylized social dilemmas, foundation model agents engaging in optimal planning consistently converge to stable cooperation, directly contradicting classical game-theoretic predictions of mutual defection. To understand this phenomenon, we introduce the `embedded Bayesian agent,' a theoretical model for foundation model agents. By shifting from decoupled to embedded agency, these agents model themselves as part of the universe they inhabit, maintaining epistemic uncertainty about their own decision-making algorithms. We show that by inferring whether others are behaviorally similar, an embedded agent treats its own deliberation during planning as evidence: a decision to cooperate predicts a similar decision by a similar partner. We formalize this mechanism of similarity inference through the `embedded equilibrium,' a novel solution concept replacing the Nash equilibrium to provide a foundational game theory for the social behavior of modern AI agents.

</details>


### [115] [Interpretable Adaptive Sampling for LLM Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03961)
*Mobina Kashaniyan,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Introduce an interpretable, adaptive test-time scaling method using a fuzzy controller that allocates sampling budgets per query based on prompt complexity and model confidence; achieves lower compute with competitive or better performance across QA and mathematical reasoning tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fixed per-query sampling budgets in test-time scaling waste compute on easy prompts and are opaque (hard to inspect why a given prompt received a certain number of samples). The paper aims to make sampling budgets per-query adaptive and interpretable, reducing wasted compute while maintaining reasoning performance.

Method: Design a lightweight fuzzy controller that takes estimated prompt complexity and model confidence as inputs and outputs a per-query number of samples; fewer samples for easy/confident prompts, more for hard/uncertain ones. Evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol (matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection) and compare with baselines (best-of-N, compute-aware scaling, self-certainty-based) on QA and math reasoning datasets; measure accuracy and average samples used.

Result: A lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals (estimated prompt complexity and model confidence) to a per-query sampling budget improves efficiency: it reduces average number of samples while matching or improving answer quality compared to standard baselines (best-of-N, compute-aware scaling, self-certainty-based methods), and stays close to full-budget selector-matched control.

Conclusion: Interpretable adaptive sampling via a fuzzy controller is a practical and effective approach for efficient test-time reasoning in LLMs, enabling inspectable compute allocation and better compute–accuracy trade-offs than fixed-budget baselines.

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves LLM reasoning by generating and aggregating multiple candidate answers, yet many pipelines use fixed per-query budgets that spend the same compute on easy and difficult prompts. These fixed budgets are also difficult to inspect because they do not explain why a given prompt receives a particular number of samples. We propose adaptive} test-time scaling with a lightweight fuzzy controller that maps interpretable signals, including estimated prompt complexity and model confidence, to a per-query sampling budget. The controller assigns fewer samples to easier or more confident prompts and more samples to harder or less certain prompts, making inference-time compute inspectable rather than fixed or opaque. We evaluate under a fair-alignment protocol with matched decoding settings and controlled answer selection, and compare against best-of-$N$, compute-aware scaling, and self-certainty-based baselines on question-answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across models and datasets, adaptive fuzzy control improves over several standard baselines and remains close to a selector-matched full-budget control while reducing the average number of samples. These findings suggest that interpretable adaptive sampling is a practical direction for more efficient test-time reasoning in large language models.

</details>


### [116] [Should We Type or Talk to LLM Agents? A Comprehensive Study of Voice and Keyboard Input Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03970)
*Zizhao Hu,Nathan Elijah Segura,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过 HIVE 仿真语音与键盘输入的扰动，发现语音转录带来的结构性改变比键盘噪声对 LLM 更具破坏性，核心机制是原 token 的保留率；轻量微调难以根治，增加推理预算能救键盘但救不了语音。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人机交互通过打字与语音两种渠道进入模型，而两者在文本层面留下不同的痕迹（键盘产生正字学噪声；语音产生口头停顿、填充词和由自动化听写/辅助录入工具引起的句子重构）。需定量理解这些渠道差异如何影响 LLM 的稳健性与实际部署表现。

Method: 构建并应用名为 HIVE 的输入扰动引擎，包含两类扰动：基于语音转录的扰动（插入停顿、填充词、重构、压缩等）与基于 QWERTY 键盘的正字学噪声扰动；将这些扰动施加到指令调优的大型语言模型的输入上，评估在多种任务（选择题与需构建/推理的开放式问题）下的性能变化，并结合 token 保留率与“思考预算”（额外推理步骤/计算）进行分析。

Result: 七项主要发现：(i) 语音转录类扰动会普遍降低指令调优模型的准确率，且关键来源是转录后的句子结构变化而非仅仅是填充词；(ii) QWERTY 键盘扰动对准确率影响较小，模型能容忍较多正字学噪声；(iii) 两类效果归因于同一机制：被扰动后原问题有多少 tokens 得以保留——删除原 token 的代价最大，额外新增 token 代价较小；(iv) 在选择题上两种渠道的差异消失，有差异的是需要构建或推理答案的场景；(v) 性能下降并非仅由测试集污染造成；(vi) 通过轻量适配无法完全消除这一弱点；(vii) 给予更多“思考预算”几乎可以完全恢复键盘通道的性能，但对语音通道无效，且对压缩语音转录反而更差。

Conclusion: 要提高模型在真实人类输入下的鲁棒性，应优先处理语音转录带来的结构性失真：改进 ASR/听写后处理、增强训练数据的结构多样性、改进 tokenization/对齐策略与更强的鲁棒训练方法；目前简单微调或仅增加推理步骤无法同时解决两类问题。

Abstract: Human input reaches language models by typing or speaking, and each channel leaves a distinct signature: orthographic noise for keyboards; for voice, disfluency from conventional transcription and restructuring from AI-backed dictation tools. How do they impact an LLM's performance? In this paper we present HIVE (Human Input-Variation Engine), a suite of voice transcription perturbations and QWERTY keyboard perturbations. We use HIVE to evaluate how robust models are to these perturbations. We present seven findings. (i) Voice transcription perturbations lower accuracy across every instruction-tuned model we test, and it is the structure of the transcription rather than its fillers that carries the cost. (ii) QWERTY keyboard perturbations cost less, and a model absorbs a lot of them before accuracy falls away. (iii) Both trace back to one cause, how many of the question's tokens survive the perturbation: destroying a token is what hurts, while adding new ones alongside it costs little. (iv) The gap between the two channels appears only where the answer must be constructed or deduced; on multiple choice there is none. (v) The harm does not solely come from test-set contamination. (vi) It cannot be trained away with lightweight adaptation. (vii) A thinking budget recovers the keyboard channel almost entirely but leaves the spoken registers untouched, and compressed speech is worse with it.

</details>


### [117] [ReflectRL: Learning from Golden Negative Trajectories via Reflective-to-Direct Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03972)
*Jinhe Bi,Chennan Zhou,Zengjie Jin,Aniri,Shuo Lu,Wenke Huang,Hu Cao,Xun Xiao,Zhihong Zhu,Volker Tresp,Fei Shen,Yunpu Ma,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Failed expert trajectories can be useful if used for reflection. ReflectRL trains models to reflect on flawed trajectories and then transfers that reflective behavior to direct problem solving, yielding robust gains in reasoning tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy training often uses golden trajectories from expert models to improve LLM reasoning, but when experts fail on harder problems those failed trajectories are discarded. The paper proposes exploiting these failed (golden negative) trajectories as signals by treating them as flawed reasoning to reflect on rather than examples to imitate.

Method: Identify failed expert trajectories; elicit Reflective Reasoning from the model using those trajectories (e.g., critique, error localization, correction); train a policy-transition mechanism to map reflective behavior into direct-answering behavior (likely via distillation, mixed loss, or constrained policy updates); integrate into existing on-policy training loops for continuous improvement.

Result: They propose ReflectRL: a lightweight framework that elicits reflective reasoning from golden negative trajectories and then performs a reflective-to-direct policy transition to transfer reasoning behavior. Across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy methods, ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning with minimal overhead.

Conclusion: Golden Negative Trajectories are a valuable supervisory signal; reflecting on flawed traces can be easier and more effective for hard problems than solving from scratch. ReflectRL is an effective, low-overhead way to exploit this insight.

Abstract: On-policy training has emerged as a powerful post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models, and is often enhanced by golden trajectories from stronger expert models. However, when the expert fails on harder problems, existing trajectory-guided methods lose their main source of supervision, and these failed trajectories are typically discarded as negative samples. We argue that such failures, which we call Golden Negative Trajectories, can still provide valuable reasoning signals when treated not as demonstrations to imitate, but as flawed trajectories to reflect upon. We identify a Reflection Advantage: for hard problems, reflecting on a flawed trajectory can be easier and more effective than solving the problem directly from scratch. Motivated by this, we propose ReflectRL, a lightweight plug-and-play framework that learns from Golden Negative Trajectories during on-policy training. ReflectRL first uses these trajectories to elicit Reflective Reasoning, then applies Reflective-to-Direct Policy Transition to transfer the acquired reasoning behavior back to Direct Reasoning. Experiments across 9 benchmarks, 4 LLM backbones, and 4 on-policy training methods show that ReflectRL consistently improves reasoning performance with minimal overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [An Empirical Analysis of the Glob Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02610)
*Phyllis Lim,Connor Adkins,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: The paper empirically demonstrates that the glob ecosystem is fragmented and security-prone, then proposes GlobSpec, a formal specification to standardize semantics and address portability, reliability, and security gaps.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Globs are pervasive but lack a formal, cross-ecosystem standard, causing fragmentation, inconsistent behaviors, security vulnerabilities, and usability problems; a rigorous study is needed to quantify these issues and guide robust solutions.

Method: Empirical mining and comparative analysis: collected data from 1,966 open-source projects, 1,355 GitHub issues, 444 CVE reports, and 361 StackOverflow posts across six ecosystems; systematically mapped feature support and real-world usage of glob implementations; categorized inconsistencies and security issues; proposed and designed a formal specification GlobSpec to standardize semantics.

Result: Identified a large feature and behavior divergence among glob implementations and a clear mismatch between implementation capabilities and developer needs; documented portability and reliability issues and numerous security vulnerabilities (about 25% of developer discussions); produced GlobSpec as a proposed formal specification to remove ambiguities and improve expressiveness.

Conclusion: Standardizing glob semantics via GlobSpec can reduce inconsistencies, improve portability and security, and better align implementations with developer requirements; the paper provides empirical evidence motivating adoption and further work to operationalize the specification.

Abstract: Glob patterns, a domain-specific language for structured string matching, are a foundational yet understudied component of modern software development embedded in everything from build scripts and configuration files to web server routes. However, this widespread use rests on a fragile foundation. Despite their ubiquity across tools and programming languages, a lack of standardization has led to a fragmented ecosystem rife with inconsistent behaviors, security vulnerabilities, and usability pitfalls. This paper presents the first empirical study of the glob ecosystem to quantify these challenges and chart a path toward robust solutions.
  Through an analysis of 1,966 open source projects, 1,355 Github issues, 444 CVE reports, and 361 StackOverflow posts, we systematically map the feature support and real-world usage of globs across six common ecosystems that utilize globs. Our findings reveal a stark divide between various globbing implementations and developer adoption. In addition, we document inconsistencies that hinder portability, reliability, and create security flaws. Our analysis reveals that security vulnerabilities are not corner cases, but a dominant concern, comprising almost a quarter of all developer discussions. We propose a path forward through standardization and introduce a formal specification for globs, GlobSpec, designed to resolve semantic ambiguities and bridge the expressiveness gap between current implementations and developer requirements.

</details>


### [119] [Rethinking Self-Evolving Agent Skills: Feedback Dynamics over Multiple Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02636)
*Yuxuan Liu,Zhaochen Su,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Zhongwei Xie,Huihao Jing,Lingyun Xie,Qing Zong,Yauwai Yim,Zhixiong Zhang,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 持久化的技能自我进化是一种稀疏、验证驱动的搜索：失败轨迹通常很关键，收益依赖模型/基准，测试时额外计算并不能一概替代进化带来的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前不清楚：何时继续让技能自我进化能带来收益；成功/失败轨迹如何分别影响修订；以及额外的测试时计算（test-time scaling）是否能替代持久化进化带来的提升。

Method: 提出一个受控评估框架：覆盖5个基准、3个模型，汇报主实验中42次反馈运行、14个模型-基准设置。在每个设置中固定执行器、优化器、修订程序、验证规则和轮次预算，仅改变呈现给优化器的反馈类型（成功+失败、仅失败、仅成功）。并通过字节不同的候选和验证选择来度量进化的持久收益；另在更广泛的SearchQA和GPT-5.5测试时间缩放对照中做补充分析。

Result: 演化极为稀疏：388个候选中仅55个在验证上实现字节不同的最优；在14个设置中有11个通过验证选择了进化技能，其中9个在released-test上表现提升。所有被选的进化解均来自包含失败轨迹的反馈条件，但Normal与Fail-only在不同设置中排名互异。验证、测试、鲁棒性、迁移评估有时偏好不同反馈视角。SearchQA的更广泛分析呈现相似的稀疏、依赖反馈的动力学。在GPT-5.5的测试时间缩放对照中，Oracle Parallel Sampling在SearchQA上接近进化技能（0.43分差），但在SpreadsheetBench上落后30.96分；Sequential Refinement未能恢复这些增益。总体而言，持久化技能自我进化更像是稀疏且受验证过滤的搜索，回报依赖于模型和基准，而非多轮持续收益。

Conclusion: 自我进化技能系统并非持续稳定改进的机制，而更像是一种稀疏、经验证筛选的搜索过程；它的收益高度依赖于模型、基准以及所呈现的反馈（尤其是失败轨迹）和验证规则。

Abstract: Self-evolving skill systems promise to improve agents by turning execution feedback into persistent skill updates without changing the underlying model. Yet it remains unclear when further evolution helps, how successful and failed trajectories shape revision, and whether extra test-time computation can recover the same gains. To address these questions, we present a controlled evaluation framework across five benchmarks and three models. Our primary study contains 42 feedback runs across 14 supported model-benchmark settings. Within each setting, we hold the executor and optimizer configuration, revision procedure, validation rule, and round budget fixed, while varying only the feedback shown to the optimizer: successes and failures (Normal), failures only, or successes only. Evolution is sparse: only 55 of 388 candidates establish byte-distinct validation bests. Validation-based selection chooses an evolved skill in 11 of 14 settings, nine of which improve released-test performance. All 11 selections come from feedback conditions that include failed trajectories, although the relative ranking of Normal and Fail-only varies across settings. Validation and downstream evaluations on test, robustness, and transfer sometimes favor different feedback views. A broader SearchQA analysis covering eight models shows similarly sparse, feedback-dependent dynamics. In the GPT-5.5 test-time-scaling controls, oracle Parallel Sampling comes within 0.43 points of the evolved SearchQA skill but remains 30.96 points behind on SpreadsheetBench; Sequential Refinement recovers neither gain. Overall, persistent skill self-evolution is better understood as sparse, validation-filtered search with model- and benchmark-dependent returns, rather than steady improvement from additional rounds. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/rethinkskill.

</details>


### [120] [Studying, Identifying, and Fixing Hidden Technical Debt in AI-Intensive Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02638)
*Beena*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本论文纲要旨在刻画AI-CPS特有的技术债务，并提出检测与偿还方法，最终实现并验证一个支持代理化AI的自动化工具以持续治理AI-CPS技术债务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI与物理系统融合的系统（AI-CPS）在自主驾驶、工业控制、家居、机器人和医疗等关键领域广泛采用，因同时包含硬件、模型与传统软件，其技术债务具有特殊性（如模型腐化、数据偏移、传感器退化、硬件-软件不匹配），对安全、可靠性和维护成本构成更高风险。亟需系统性刻画、检测与可自动化偿还方法。

Method: 混合方法：定性与定量相结合。首先通过分析AI生态系统（开发工具、数据、模型）和AI-CPS代码/配置/镜像仓库来归纳技术债项类型；并通过对从业开发者的半结构化访谈来补充场景化证据与因果机制。基于特征和根源，设计若干检测/度量方法（静态分析、运行时监测、元数据分析）以及偿还策略（重构、数据清理、模型再训练、治理规则）。最后实现并验证一个支持自治/代理型AI的自动化工具，用于持续监测、治理与偿还AI-CPS技术债务，并在实验系统或案例研究中评估其有效性与开销。

Result: 预期产出包括：1）一套AI-CPS技术债务分类与特征（类型、触发条件、影响面）；2）多模态检测与度量方法（静态仓库分析+运行时/数据流监测）；3）若干偿还策略与治理机制原型；4）一个实现的代理化工具，并通过案例研究/实验评估其检测准确率、修复有效性与性能开销。

Conclusion: 该研究将填补AI-CPS在技术债务识别与自动化偿还方面的空白，提升关键领域系统的可维护性与可靠性。后续工作可扩展到多组织协同治理、法规约束集成以及更广泛的自动化补救策略库。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) components are increasingly pervasive in several software systems, including Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). AI-CPS are used in several domains, including autonomous vehicles, industry, home automation, robotics, and healthcare. Being composed of hardware, AI components, and conventional modules, AI-CPS can exhibit technical debt (TD) that is peculiar and potentially more challenging than that of conventional systems. This thesis aims to characterize AI-CPS TD and propose approaches for its identification and repair. In a first phase, we characterize AI-CPS TD by analyzing AI ecosystems and AI-CPS repositories, as well as interviewing developers. Based on the acquired knowledge, we define approaches to identify and mitigate such TD. Finally, we plan to develop and validate an automated tool that supports agentic AI solutions to monitor, govern, and repay AI-CPS TD.

</details>


### [121] [Instruction Stacking Collapse: A Benchmark and the Capability-Dependent Value of Prompt Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02639)
*Atul Anand,Sourav Chattaraj*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 作者构建了一个堆叠最多24条经验证器检验的指令的基准（每次1–20条），评估三款生产级大模型，发现指令遵从率非线性下降（约从96%降到20%），由一组可重复的成对冲突驱动。提出了一个无需训练的“指令编译器”——在单次模型调用中重写堆叠提示——对较弱模型最多恢复+11个百分点的遵从率，较强模型影响不大；并公开了基准、验证器与缓存结果用于复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的系统提示通常同时包含多种约束（如JSON格式、字数限制、引用数量、语气等）。需要量化多重约束积累对指令遵从的影响，并寻找无需再训练即可在部署端缓解此问题的实用方案。

Method: 构建含24条可验证指令的基准集，按1–20条不同深度堆叠进行测试；用自动验证器评估输出是否遵从；对Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5-mini、Gemini 2.5 Flash三款模型运行；设计并复用一个训练免费（prompt rewrite）指令编译器，在单次LMM调用中将堆叠提示重写为等价但更可执行的形式；用簇健壮性测试、相同基线控制和同族规模梯度分析，验证改进来源于重写本身。

Result: 遵从率从约96%非线性下降到最低约20%；发现一组可重复的成对冲突（例如“输出JSON”与其他9条约束存在联合不可满足性）；指令编译器对较弱模型可带来最多+11个百分点的遵从率提升，且对强模型几乎无影响；多项对照证明改进源于重写，而非额外令牌或测量空间；基准、验证器和缓存运行结果已公开。

Conclusion: 堆叠多条生产提示会引发结构化、可复现的成对冲突，导致遵从性能以非线性方式恶化；通过将多重约束在一次调用中重写为一致、可执行的指令，可以显著改善较弱模型的表现，且效果不是由额外令牌或重排序引起。

Abstract: Production prompts rarely carry a single instruction. One system message may require valid JSON, a word limit, three citations, and a fixed tone at the same time. We study how instruction-following degrades as such constraints accumulate. We introduce a benchmark that stacks 24 verifier-checked instructions, one to twenty at a time, and evaluate three production-tier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5-mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash). Instruction-following degrades non-linearly: the follow rate falls from ~96% to as low as 20%, driven by a structured and reproducible set of pairwise conflicts. A single "output JSON" constraint, for example, is jointly unsatisfiable with nine others. We then evaluate a training-free remedy: an instruction compiler that rewrites the stacked prompt in a single LLM call and is reused across queries. Its benefit is capability-graded. It recovers up to +11 points of follow rate for weaker models, which are also the models most often deployed at scale, while leaving stronger models, which already internalise the same structure, essentially unchanged. Cluster-robust tests, same-baseline controls, and a within-family scaling ladder attribute the gain to the rewrite itself rather than to additional tokens, reordering, or measurement headroom. We release the benchmark, verifiers, and cached runs for full reproduction.

</details>


### [122] [IR2Solve: Structured Intermediate Representations for Cost-Efficient Optimization Autoformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02641)
*Penglin Zhu,Linhai Zhang,Jungang Xu,Xinchi Wei,Xiuqi Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 IR2Solve：一次语义 LLM 调用生成受限 ModelIR，后接确定性验证与 IR→求解器编译。标量约束约定减少索引/量化错误，在正确性与调用/token 成本上优于多轮方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生成求解器代码脆弱：模式、索引与语义错误会导致编译失败、不适定模型或错误目标；基于多轮修复、搜索或多代理的方案尽管能提高准确率但极大地增加推理调用数和 token 成本。作者提出通过把复杂语义问题约束到结构化中间表示并把可检测的错误放到确定性后处理来减少 LLM 交互次数与失败率。

Method: （1）设计 ModelIR 模式：明确定义集合、参数、变量、目标、约束的受限 Python 风格表达语法；（2）使用单次语义 LLM 调用将自然语言优化问题映射为符合模式的 ModelIR；（3）确定性验证：检查符号/类型、集合与索引的一致性、约束的标量化（展开每个索引实例）、目标与约束可行性/尺度合理性等；（4）IR→求解器编译器：将经验证的 ModelIR 逐条转换为求解器 API/建模语言（例如 Pyomo/CPLEX/GLPK）或求解器接受的数学表达。

Result: IR2Solve 提出了一种“先中间表示（IR）”的自动建模流水线：使用一次语义级 LLM 调用生成受限的 ModelIR（显式表示集合、参数、变量、目标与约束，采用类似 Python 的受限表达式字符串），随后以两个确定性阶段完成验证与把 IR 编译为求解器输入。引入了“标量约束”约定（将基于索引的约束族展开为每个标量约束条目），从而减少自由索引与隐式量化错误并简化验证与编译。实证上，在六个清洗后的基准上实现了较强的目标正确性并与近期系统竞争；在 153 个 IndustryOR 与 ComplexLP 实例的消融实验中展示了结构化 IR、标量约束指令与确定性验证带来的逐步改进；在 10 个匹配成本样例上，IR2Solve 每实例只需一次语义调用，而 Chain-of-Experts 与 SAC-Opt 分别需 8 与 39 次调用，总 token 消耗分别为 IR2Solve 的 3.3× 与 22.9×。总体展示出以结构化 IR + 确定性后处理为核心的准确性-成本折衷。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示加上规则化的标量约束约定与确定性后处理，可显著降低 LLM 自动建模中的语义与索引错误，同时在推理成本上远优于多轮交互或多模型工作流，使得单次语义调用的自动配方在实用精度-成本权衡上成为可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can translate natural-language optimization problems into solver-ready formulations, but direct code generation is brittle: schema, indexing, and semantic errors can cause compilation failures, infeasible models, or incorrect objectives, while iterative repair, search, and multi-agent workflows increase inference cost. We present IR2Solve, an intermediate-representation-first autoformulation pipeline that uses a single semantic LLM call to produce a schema-constrained ModelIR, followed by two deterministic stages: verification and IR-to-solver compilation. ModelIR explicitly represents sets, parameters, variables, objectives, and constraints using restricted Python-like expression strings. A concrete scalar-constraint convention represents finite per-index constraint families as individual entries, reducing free-index and implicit-quantification errors while simplifying downstream verification and compilation. Across six cleaned optimization benchmarks, IR2Solve achieves strong objective correctness and remains competitive with recent optimization-modeling systems. A controlled ablation on 153 IndustryOR and ComplexLP instances shows sequential gains from the structured IR interface, the scalar-constraint instruction, and deterministic verification. On a matched ten-instance cost panel, IR2Solve uses one semantic call per instance, whereas Chain-of-Experts and SAC-Opt use 8 and 39 calls per instance and consume 3.3 and 22.9 times the token volume of IR2Solve, respectively. These results show that structured intermediate representations, combined with deterministic post-generation processing, provide a practical accuracy-cost trade-off for LLM-based optimization autoformulation.

</details>


### [123] [CUADebug: Diagnosing and Repairing Computer-Use Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02643)
*Weijia Zhang,Kunlun Zhu,Zeyi Liu,Yinting Chen,Tianyi Ma,Jiateng Liu,Jiaxun Zhang,Bingxuan Li,Xiangru Tang,Heng Ji,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CUADebug 框架（错误分类 + OSWorld 基准 + 工具化调试器），通过主动检查关键步骤并提交结构化根因诊断，实现了 CUA 故障定位与可操作修复的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与纯文本代理不同，CUA 的失败源自视觉感知、空间接地、低级交互、任务推理和环境动态的耦合，导致传统单模因果定位方法不足，需要专门的框架来诊断并生成可执行修复策略。

Method: CUADebug 包含：1）CUA 专用错误分类法，覆盖任务推理、感知、接地/交互、外部/系统等故障家族；2）CUAErrorBench：在 OSWorld 环境上人工标注的 204 条失败轨迹基准；3）CUADebugger：以成对的前/后截图和动作轨迹主动检查可疑步骤，提交结构化诊断（根因步骤、错误类型、落地证据、修正策略），并以该诊断指导重执行与持续修复。评估使用多个大模型调试后端（如 Gemini 2.5 Pro、Claude）并对比历史延续、机器 RCA 与人工 oracle。

Result: CUADebug 提出并验证了一个面向计算机界面代理（CUA）故障诊断与修复的框架。通过构建 CUA 专用错误分类、人工标注的 OSWorld 失败基准（CUAErrorBench）以及交互式调试工具 CUADebugger，论文实现了在根因定位与可执行修复上的显著提升：在 Claude-agent 划分上，联合子类与步骤诊断从 11.2% 提升到 19.6%（使用 Gemini 2.5 Pro）；单次重试包评估中，基于 RCA 的条件比仅历史延续的任务完成率更高（机器 RCA 28.47%，作者方法 29.90%，历史延续 13.89%）；持续重试时成功率从 12.2% 提升到 25.86%，而人工 oracle 指导达到 29.21%。

Conclusion: CUA 故障具有多模态耦合特性，基于根因的定位（RCA）不仅能提供解释性信息，更能产生可执行的修复策略，从而显著提升自动代理在界面任务上的恢复能力；但当前方法仍离人工指导上限有差距，且受限于基准环境和注释质量。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) operate real desktop and web interfaces through screenshots, mouse and keyboard actions, and stateful UI feedback, yet their failures remain difficult to diagnose and repair. Unlike text-only agents, CUA failures arise from coupled visual perception, spatial grounding, low-level interaction, task reasoning, and environment dynamics, making debugging a distinctive multimodal causal localization problem. We introduce CUADebug, a framework for diagnosing and repairing CUA failures. CUADebug includes a CUA-specific error taxonomy, CUAErrorBench, a human-annotated OSWorld failure benchmark, and CUADebugger, a tool-augmented debugger. Instead of prompting over the full trajectory once, CUADebugger actively inspects suspicious steps with paired before/after screenshots and action traces, then submits a structured diagnosis containing the root-cause step, error type, grounded evidence, and corrective strategy for re-execution. Human annotations over 204 failed trajectories show that task reasoning and control is the largest failure family (110/204), followed by perception (36), grounding/interaction (25), external/system (13), and an others category of 20 OSWorld infeasible-task cases. On the main Claude-agent split, CUADebugger improves joint subtype-and-step diagnosis from 11.2% to 19.6% with Gemini 2.5 Pro and improves consistently across debugger backbones. In single re-execution package evaluation, RCA-based conditions achieve higher task completion than history-only continuation (28.47% with machine RCA and 29.90% with our method, versus 13.89%); in continual re-execution, our method improves success from 12.2% to 25.86%, while human-oracle guidance reaches 29.21%. These results show that CUA root-cause diagnosis can provide actionable repair signals rather than merely post-hoc explanations.

</details>


### [124] [A Unified Feature Model for Microservice Identification and Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02644)
*Ana Almeida,António Rito Silva*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一套描述微服务识别工具可变点的特征模型，揭示了现有工具的互补性与设计空间的分散性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前微服务识别方法在数据收集、分析、分解算法和可视化/精化机制上差异较大，缺乏通用概念框架阻碍系统性比较与集成；研究表明没有单一最优方法，需要多方法集成来权衡设计选择。

Method: 基于对现有工作的广泛分析，构建一份面向多变体微服务识别工具的特征模型；通过系统映射（systematic mapping）对代表性文献进行评估，并将模型实例化到一个现有工具的架构中进行验证。

Result: 特征模型覆盖了主要变体点且能作为统一分析框架；文献和工具分析显示：各工具各自实现模型的部分功能，但合并后几乎覆盖全部变体点，揭示了工具间的互补性与碎片化问题。

Conclusion: 提出的特征模型有效捕捉了现有微服务识别方法的主要可变点，为比较和设计工具提供了统一基础；尽管单个工具无法覆盖全部变体，但这些工具合起来几乎涵盖了模型的所有变体点，表明设计空间已被填充但分散在独立工具中。
